## 6.13.20 - 2026-11-17

- Fixed (`stack/local-up.mts`, `stack-local-config.mts`): a seed command of `local.seed` takes `mode: once | always` (default once). An `always` command (a migration runner idempotent by its own ledger, such as TypeORM's `migrate-typeorm`) runs on every `starci app stack up`, so a new migration reaches an existing stack; a `once` command keeps its receipt per volume set. Before, every seed command ran once per volume set and a new TypeORM migration never ran on a stack that already had one.
- Fixed (`db/migrate.mts`): an absent `db/migrations` (or `database.migrationsDir`) is an empty chain, no longer a refusal; `stack up` no longer stops at `db migrate` with a silent exit 2 on an app that has no SQL migration yet. A directory that exists but cannot be read is still `DB_MIGRATIONS_MISSING`.
- Fixed (`stack/local-up.mts`): a failed `db migrate` under `stack up` keeps its report and `STACK_LOCAL_MIGRATE` names its first coded line (the database URL redacted) instead of a bare exit code.
- Changed: the dev README of the lite template says an absent `db/migrations` is an empty chain and when to declare `mode: always`; `contexts/project/stacks.md` PROJECT-STACKS-6.
- Specs: `stack-local.spec.mts` (always vs once, the mode judged, the coded migrate failure), `db-migrate-phase.spec.mts` (absent folder).
- Changed (`knowledge/hfs/canon-pins.yaml`): the `@starci/hfs` self-pin is 6.13.20.

## 6.13.19 - 2026-11-17

- Added: the bring-up loader of the pinned seed-standards (`stack/standard-load.mts`, PROJECT-STACKS-13 "The loader"). After the R434 gate, `seedStep` reads each pinned `data.sets` seed-standard of the environment at its commit (`pinRepository`, then the git driver of @starci/seed over the same mirror: its verify and its read) and applies it through the app's seed door, each store's `adapter.command` over `starci/data-entries@1` (`commandStore`, run by the seed step's own command runner): a dry run through every door first (`restoreFrom`, mode mirror, nothing written), then an upsert by natural key (mode merge). One receipt per standard and commit (scope `standard`, id `<set>@<commit>`, hash the export digest), written only once every store applied: a receipt skips the pin, a first pin applies, a changed pin is reported `DATA_STANDARD_CHANGED` and applies only with the set's `force: true`, once per commit; what the standard no longer names is reported and left. A set with `completeness: ext:` is left to the project. New refusals: `STACK_SEED_STANDARD_UNREADABLE`, `STACK_SEED_STANDARD_SCHEMA`, `STACK_SEED_STORE_COMMAND`, `STACK_SEED_DOOR_REFUSED` (`modules/kernel/failure-codes.yaml`, R435 `failureCodes`, enforcer `standard-load`).
- Changed: `seedStep` is async (`Promise<ActionResult>`); `stack up` and the deploy's seed step await it. The receipts read is ordered by `applied_at`. `StandardPin` carries the set's `stores` and `force`; the deploy plan line says "verify and load".
- Changed (R435): `HFS_DATA_SECOND_LOADER` is an ERROR again, no longer report-only: the loader ships, so an app has its lawful path in. An app that still syncs its content another way (a content-sync command, a clone of a `-seed-standard` repository) fails `starci app check` until it declares the standard in `data.sets` and removes that path.
- Changed (R426, `rules/data-stores.mts`): a store a seed-standard fills (no project `completeness`) needs `adapter.command`, its seed door, like a store of a seed-backup set (`DATA_STORE_ADAPTER`).
- Fixed (R434): the adapter the guard runs from a commit hook, and the git and npm the bring-up gate runs in its scratch checkout and over the pin's mirror, no longer inherit `GIT_*` variables (`withoutGitEnvironment` in `rules/data-adapter.mts`, `pinRunner` in `stack/standard-gate.mts`). Under a hook (GIT_DIR, GIT_INDEX_FILE, GIT_WORK_TREE set) the git that `renderStandard` runs in its scratch copy committed into the user's branch and rewrote its index.
- Specs: `standard-load.spec.mts` (new), `data-adapter.spec.mts` (a real pre-commit hook), `data-loaders.spec.mts`, `data-stores.spec.mts`, `stack-seeds.spec.mts`.
- Changed (`knowledge/hfs/canon-pins.yaml`): the `@starci/hfs` self-pin is 6.13.19.

Pin 6.13.18, not 6.13.17.
- Changed (CONTRIB-20, publish.yml, `.github/scripts/publish-gate.mts`): nothing publishes unless the whole ci run of the commit is green, the Sonar quality gate and the app job included; publish.yml starts only on a successful ci and the gate holds every package and the CLI when any job of the run is not green (spec: `cli/src/core/release/publish-workflow.test.ts`).

## 6.13.17 - 2026-11-17

- Security (`knowledge/hfs/canon-pins.yaml`, owner-approved): `next` 16.4.5, which fixes the advisories published 2026-11-03 that affect 16.x up to 16.4.4: GHSA-pc58-hwjq-p385, GHSA-rhh4-g77r-r9qj, GHSA-vvgp-ww4w-46vf, GHSA-7qx7-33qx-rqx2, GHSA-mfr8-8qww-v76h, GHSA-vc7v-qv4w-wrv8, GHSA-f3mh-rxg3-f5g2. An app on 16.4.2 reports HFS_CANON_PIN_DRIFT until it moves.
- Changed (R435): `HFS_DATA_SECOND_LOADER` is REPORT-ONLY (a warning) in this version, because the bring-up loader that applies the pinned `data.sets` standards is not built yet (stacks.md "Known gap") and an app that still syncs its content another way (`cli content sync`) has no lawful path in. It becomes an error in the release that ships the loader.
- Added: R434 (`rules/data-adapter.mts`, `stack/standard-gate.mts`, PROJECT-STACKS-13): a seed-standard may keep what people author in `raw/` and an `adapter/` that converts it through the git driver of @starci/seed. The copy repository's commit hook and CI (`starci app check --staged`, `sync/hygiene.mts`) run `node adapter/main.ts` twice over raw/ as the index holds it and compare its output by blob id with the committed manifest.json, data/, blobs/ and index/: a failed run, two runs that differ, a hand edit of data/, raw/ committed without its output and an unstaged adapter/ change are `HFS_DATA_ADAPTER_DRIFT`; an adapter repository without adapter/main.ts, adapter/package.json, `.githooks/pre-commit` or `.github/workflows/seed-standard.yml` running the guard is `HFS_DATA_ADAPTER_UNGUARDED`.
- Added: the seed step verifies each pinned seed-standard of the environment once per pin before any entry applies (`pinRepository` into `<state base>/data/standards`, the adapter installed from its lockfile in a scratch checkout, the R434 drift check; receipt scope `standard`, hash the commit) and refuses a drifting or unreadable pin `STACK_SEED_ADAPTER_DRIFT`; `hasSeedEntries` and the deploy's seed plan line count the pins.
- Added: R435 (`rules/data-loaders.mts`, an app check finder): `HFS_DATA_SECOND_LOADER` refuses an app that reads a `-seed-standard` repository by another path than the seed step: a git clone, pull, fetch, archive, submodule, sparse-checkout or remote add of one (kind git), a content-sync command or a script naming one (kind command), an environment variable naming one or a SEED_STANDARD name (kind env); ext/, .starciwork, Markdown, lock files and the data.sets declaration are not judged.
- Changed (R400, `rules/data-copies.mts`): raw/ is sized (4 MiB a file) and capped (the 500 MiB of the snapshot) like the data files, and a manifest files index naming anything but a data file is `HFS_DATA_MANIFEST_DRIFT`.
- Changed (`knowledge/hfs/canon-pins.yaml`): `@starci/seed` 0.2.0 (`renderStandard`, `runStandardAdapter`); the `@starci/hfs` self-pin is 6.13.17, the package version.
- Added: the R434 and R435 rows of `knowledge/hfs/rules.yaml`, their failure codes in `modules/kernel/failure-codes.yaml` and `A15_CODES`; specs `data-adapter.spec.mts`, `data-loaders.spec.mts` and the raw/ case of `data-copies.spec.mts`.

- Changed (`knowledge/hfs/canon-pins.yaml`): `@starci/ai-router` 0.10.0 (the `local` provider kind: a self-hosted OpenAI-compatible GPU endpoint such as Ollama, keyless allowed, cost 0, MRL embedding truncation) and `@starci/test-world` 2.2.1 (a run shrinks its slots when Redis databases run out instead of failing). The Grammar family 0.40.1 (Switch `kind="toggle" | "compact"`) is admitted by the existing `~0.40.0` range.

## 6.13.11 - 2026-11-16

- Added (R395 `HFS_APP_GALLERY_ROUTE_MISSING`, `runtime/scripts/hfs/gallery-routes.mts`, `rules/gallery.mts`): every user-facing route of the front end, a `page.tsx` under `fe/apps/<app>/src/app` (the `[locale]` prefix, `(group)` folders, parallel slots, intercepting routes and private folders are not part of a route; a dynamic segment is one screen), needs at least one screen folder in `gallery/pages` whose `explain.md` front matter `route` matches it (the locale prefix, a trailing slash and the name of a dynamic parameter are ignored). Coverage is judged from the explain.md files alone: the shots stay git-ignored local evidence, so shots saved elsewhere cover nothing. A route that is not a product screen is declared in the new `gallery/routes.json` (`{ "exclude": [{ "route", "reason" }] }`, slot `app.gallery-routes`); an exclusion with no reason, a malformed file or an exclusion of a route the front end does not have is `HFS_APP_GALLERY_LINKS`. The code is `localOnly`: a warning in `--env local`, an error in `--env prod` (the push hook, CI, the release gate), the split `HFS_APP_GALLERY_REGION_UNCLEAR` uses. An app with a gallery and no `gallery/pages` (nivoos-lite) now fails `--env prod` for every route.
- Added (R395 `HFS_APP_GALLERY_EXPLAIN_STALE`, `rules/gallery-coupling.mts`): a change to a screen's source, the route's `page.tsx` and every file under the `features/pages/<Name>/` folder it imports (best effort: a component a feature takes from `components/` is not followed), that leaves the screen's `explain.md` untouched. `starci app check` (a whole run) judges the files the commits since the base, the index and the work tree change, with the base the lint ratchet uses (`STARCI_BASE_REF`, `origin/<branch>`, else `HEAD^`); the pre-commit guard `starci app check --staged` lists it for the staged files and never blocks. A route with several screen folders is current when any of their explain.md is touched; an excluded route and a route with no screen have nothing to keep current. `localOnly`, as above.
- Changed (`scaffold/app.mts`, `templates/app/skeleton*/gallery/pages`): a scaffold writes the starter screens of its routes (lite: the front door and the five email doors; full: the product app and the landing, an app with no front end writes none), each an `explain.md` with real regions, so a fresh app passes the coverage. `starci app sync` renders the managed gallery only, as before: an existing app writes its own screens.
- Changed (contexts): `contexts/project/gallery.md` PROJECT-GALLERY-4 (every route has a screen, exclusions) and PROJECT-GALLERY-5 (a screen's explanation changes with its source) and the R395 "Enforced by"; `contexts/hfs/rules.yaml` and `runtime/knowledge/hfs/rules.yaml` R395, the slot `app.gallery-routes` in both slot manifests, the failure texts (Vietnamese cause and next step) in `failure-codes.yaml`.
- Tests: `src/app-gallery-routes.spec.mts` (route and key derivation, routes.json shape, coverage passing and violating in both editions, dynamic parameter names, exclusions, shots without explain.md, the coupling over a changed page and feature folder, the local and prod split, the staged guard and the range since a base), `src/app-gallery.spec.mts` (a scaffold's starter screens, sync of an app without pages).
- Upgrade: an app with a gallery writes `gallery/pages/<area>/<screen>/explain.md` for each route (or declares the developer-only ones in `gallery/routes.json`) before its next `--env prod` check; `--env local` lists what is missing.

## 6.13.7 - 2026-11-16

- Fixed (R47 `BE_TEST_TOPOLOGY`, BE-TEST-12, new `rules/test-infra-placement.mts`): a homemade test world no longer passes. hfs reads the tracked tree (TypeScript syntax trees, imports resolved through the side's tsconfig) and refuses, outside `src/tests/world/`, a file under `src/tests/` that imports `@nestjs/testing`, an app's `AppModule` or `app.module`, `NestFactory`, `testcontainers`, `@testcontainers/*`, `dockerode` or `docker-modem`, or calls `createTestingModule` or `.listen(` ("test infrastructure lives only in src/tests/world/ (@starci/test-world)"); and any `overrideProvider`, `overrideGuard`, `overrideModule`, `overrideInterceptor`, `overrideFilter` or `overridePipe` call under `src/tests/` or in any integration, e2e or contract spec (the machine half of `tests-no-override`, which an app that lints nothing on its dev branch never ran). The slot `be.tests.fixtures` is documented as data and builders only.
- Added (R47 `BE_SPEC_WORLD_MISSING`): every `*.integration-spec.ts` and `*.e2e-spec.ts` of a back end, in both editions, imports `useTestWorld` from the repository's own `src/tests/world/use-test-world` and calls it; a world assembled in a fixture (a `bootWorld`) or imported from elsewhere is refused. Until now R112 asked for `useTestWorld` only of an integration provider slot, and an app with no such slot was never asked. Failure text in `failure-codes.yaml` (Vietnamese cause and next step).
- Changed (R212, R272, SHARED-TEST-3, BE-TEST-12, `project/lite.md` "What CI runs"): a lite app has the e2e slot. `src/tests/e2e/<flow>/*.e2e-spec.ts` boots the full app(s) through `useTestWorld({ apps })` on the local db service, seeds a real package through the app's deploy door, drives the app only through its external doors (HTTP, a webhook, GraphQL), fakes only at the network edge and asserts persisted state; it runs only on request (R12) and never replaces the wiring proof (R272). Integration stays one module or capability through `useTestWorld({ modules })`. The slot `be.tests.e2e` is no longer `litePresence: forbidden`; `test:e2e` is no longer a forbidden lite script (`HFS_EDITION_FORBIDDEN_PRESENT` keeps `test:world`, `test:browser`, containers, toxiproxy and browser runners); the lite `be.tests.world` entry exports `{ apps }` too.
- Tests: `src/test-infra-placement.spec.mts` (a homemade world and an override are refused, the world, builders and specs on `useTestWorld` pass, both editions and a monorepo side prefix; a lite e2e flow and `test:e2e` pass the check), `src/hfs-edition-forbidden-present.spec.mts` (lite keeps refusing `test:world` and containers).
- Upgrade: an app with a boot helper under `src/tests/fixtures/` declares `src/tests/world/` (`@starci/test-world` README), moves the infrastructure there and calls `useTestWorld` from its specs; a lite app may add `test:e2e` and `src/tests/e2e/<flow>/`.


## 6.13.6 - 2026-11-16

- Changed (R12 `HFS_E2E_IN_AUTOMATIC_GATE`, `contexts/project/cicd.md` "Triggers and what runs"): the integration, e2e and contract suites run only when a person asks for them, never automatically and never as part of CI. The release gate no longer has `test-integration`, `test-e2e` and `test-contract` among its default rows (`defaultGates` of `release/gate.mts`); a row an app declares of its own under `release.gates` is the app's choice and still runs. No hook, coverage run, workflow or default gate row runs a world layer; `npm run test:e2e` (and `test:integration`, `test:contract`) is how a person asks.
- Added (CI standard item `no-world-layers`, R301 `CI_STANDARD`, `knowledge/hfs/ci-standard.yaml`): a `run` step of any workflow, the managed `ci.yml` of an app and the runtime's own workflows alike, that runs a world layer is refused: jest `--selectProjects integration|e2e|contract`, a `*.integration-spec`, `*.e2e-spec` or `*.contract-spec` path, `test:e2e`, `test:integration` or `test:contract`, or an `e2e`, `integration` or `contract` npm script (npm, pnpm, yarn). The finding carries a Vietnamese cause and next step in `failure-codes.yaml`.
- Changed (ci-standard.yaml `boot-proof`): the standard no longer says that a full app "keeps its e2e boot proof": no CI runs the e2e suite, and the boot proof CI keeps is the wiring gate (`starci app check --gate wiring`, R272) where an app has one.
- Changed (texts): R12, slot `app.ci-e2e`, the sync note for `.github/workflows/e2e.yml`, the `HFS_E2E_IN_AUTOMATIC_GATE` failure text and the scaffold comments say "on request only" in place of "by hand and as the world rows of the release gate".
- Tests: `src/ci-no-world-layers.spec.mts` (a workflow step running an e2e, integration or contract suite is refused, a unit-only step passes; the managed `ci.yml`, the hooks and every managed workflow of both editions run none), and the release gate specs (`release-gate-reuse`, `no-loosening-ci`) assert the world rows are no default row. No template changed, so no template snapshot moves.
- Upgrade: an app re-renders its `ci.yml` with `starci app sync --execute` (a no-op for a CI that already ran no world layer); a release gate that relied on the default `test-e2e` rows declares them under `release.gates` or runs `npm run test:e2e` itself when it wants them.

## 6.13.5 - 2026-11-16

- Added (BE-PERSISTENCE-20, R431 `db-key-shape`, `runtime/scripts/hfs/rules/database-keys.mts`): the key shape of an app table, judged over the active migrations of `db/migrations` in both editions. `DB_PRIMARY_KEY_SHAPE`: a created table's primary key is `id uuid` (a natural key is a unique constraint beside it). `DB_FOREIGN_KEY_NAME`: a one-column foreign key is `<referenced table singular>_id` or `<role>_<referenced table singular>_id`, or the table's own `id` when it shares the key of the row it extends; the singular is a fixed rule set on the last word (`ies` y, `sses`/`xes`/`ches`/`shes` less `es`, a trailing `s` dropped, a word in `ss`, `us` or `is` kept) after the app's map of irregular plurals, new hfs.json `db.singulars`. `DB_FOREIGN_KEY_INDEX`: every foreign key has an index, primary key or unique constraint leading with its columns somewhere in the migration set. New hfs.json `db.keyExceptions` (`{ table, reason }`) exempts a table a package or a natural key keys from the shape and name checks, never from the index check. The three codes join the landed known items (`database-landed.mts`): a migration already on the base unchanged is the backlog, reported at level info, and every new migration blocks. Stamps strictly increase (`DB_MIGRATION_SHAPE`) and a destructive change belongs to a `*_contract_*` migration (`DB_MIGRATION_DESTRUCTIVE`) as before; disabling RLS or removing FORCE stays `DB_RLS_REQUIRED`.
- Changed (templates, R431): the table generator's owner column is `owner_user_id` (migration, index, policies, entity column; the entity property stays `ownerId`), and `requireOwnedEntity` of the lite sql helpers reads the owner through the entity property `owned.ownerId`, so an older table's `owner_id` and a new table's `owner_user_id` are both owned; `app add table` moves an app's existing copy of the helper to the property. The platform inbox (`public.inbox`) and the job ticks (`public.job_ticks`) are keyed by `id uuid` with their natural keys `unique (source, event_id)` and `unique (name)`, which their `on conflict` targets keep.
- Added (BE-RESILIENCE-2, R432 `failure-class`, `rules/failure-class.mts`): `BE_FAILURE_CLASS_BY_TEXT` refuses a back-end source (`src/**`, not a spec, a declaration or a test folder) that matches a transient failure code (the `TRANSIENT_CODES` of `@starci/nest-kit` and their siblings) as a quoted literal or inside a regular expression literal: the class is set on the thrown error as `failureClass` and judged by `classifyFailure` alone; a back end with a Postgres job outbox takes it from `@starci/outbox`.
- Added (PROJECT-WORK-7, R433 `concept-conventions`, `rules/concepts-conventions.mts`): `HFS_CONCEPT_CONVENTIONS_MISSING` refuses an app root that tracks no `.starciwork/concepts/conventions.md` or whose catalog `.starciwork/concepts/README.md` has no row linking it. Both skeletons render the concept and the catalog row, and the catalog's opening says to read `.starcistacks/application-stacks.yaml` whole and the environment README first.
- Changed: the canon pin of `@starci/mail` is 0.3.0 (`runtime/knowledge/hfs/canon-pins.yaml`, `contexts/stack/pins.yaml`): Cc, Bcc, In-Reply-To, References and a named Message-ID on send, and a CRLF in a header value refused.
- Upgrade, per existing app: write `.starciwork/concepts/conventions.md` and its catalog row (R433); a lite app that copied `@starci/outbox`'s `migrations/outbox.sql` lists `public.outbox_slots` in hfs.json `db.keyExceptions` with its reason when the copy is a new migration; an app whose `requireOwnedEntity` still reads `owned.owner_id` moves it to `owned.ownerId` before its next generated table (`app add table` does it).

## 6.13.4 - 2026-11-15

- Changed: the canon pin of `@starci/auth` is 0.7.1 (`runtime/knowledge/hfs/canon-pins.yaml`, `contexts/stack/pins.yaml`): a refresh cookie left by an earlier deployment (host-only, another domain, a legacy path) is cleared at sign-in and on a refusal, so it never shadows the new session.

## 6.13.3 - 2026-11-15

- Fixed (`stack deploy --urgent`, contexts/project/cicd.md "The urgent path" and "Database migrations"): a deploy's safety steps run where the data is, whichever path triggered the deploy. The urgent floor now keeps only what an urgent release adds (the pushed urgent record, the rollback target, the public verify) and then runs the deploy's own database phases (`stack/db-phases.mts`), in the same place and under the same codes as the automatic path. For a `database.target: stack` database the migrate preview and the migrations run inside its container, and the backup is made, receipted and test-decrypted ON THE MANAGER through the deploy's ssh runner with the server's identity: the operator's machine needs no PostgreSQL client, no route to the database, no `DATABASE_URL` and no `SOPS_AGE_KEY_FILE` (was: `STACK_DEPLOY_BACKUP_FAILED (DB_CLI_MISSING)` on a machine without them). The floor's one difference stays: its backup is taken even when nothing is pending (`alwaysBackup`). An `external` database is backed up where the deploy runs, as before. The expand migrations run before the deploy and the contract ones after the accept (was: every migration before the deploy).
- Fixed (`stack deploy --urgent --first-start`, PROJECT-STACKS-6): the urgent first start gives the app role its sealed password (`database.appPassword`, `hfs.json` `db.appRole`) after the migrations, exactly as the automatic first start; it omitted it, so the api booted into "password authentication failed". An empty `stack` database takes the first start by itself on the urgent path too. `--db-service` of a `stack` database may be omitted (its declared service) and is refused when it names another (`STACK_FIRST_START_USAGE`). A red first-start step keeps its own code on both paths.
- Fixed (PROJECT-STACKS-12): the urgent path runs the seed step after convergence, as every deploy does; it skipped it silently. Its dry run names the backup's place and every seed entry.
- Fixed (`app secret set --generate`): a missing value says `--generate needs a value: --generate <bytes> (a whole byte count from 1 to 1024, e.g. --generate 32)`, and a value that is no byte count says that the value after `--generate` is the byte count.
- Upgrade: an app takes this release by raising `@starci/hfs` to 6.13.3 and `starci-harness` to the CLI release that requires it. Before its next urgent deploy of a `stack` database the manager needs `age` and the server identity (`starci app secret server-key`), as the automatic path's backup already does; drop `--db-url-env` for a `stack` database (refused `STACK_DEPLOY_DB_URL_UNEXPECTED`).

## 6.13.2 - 2026-11-15

- Fixed (PROJECT-LINT-4; R13, R269, R298, R301, R303): an app that declares gates off is compliant with the managed files `app sync` renders for it. Every rule that requires a gate's step in the managed CI reads the same declaration (`declaredGatesOff`, `IDLE_JOBS` and `idleJobs` in `runtime/scripts/lib/stack-declaration.mts`): `HFS_CI_MISSING_CANON` needs no lint step while lint is off, `HFS_AUDIT_STEP_MISSING` no audit step while audit is off, `CI_STANDARD` no coverage proof (coverage), boot proof (wiring) or sharded unit run (test) while that gate is off, `CI_DECLARATION_DRIFT` no workflow read of the names a service binds whose id is a gate declared off (the Sonar token and host), and `HFS_BYPASS_MARKER` accepts the constant false `if` of a job of the managed ci.yml that the declaration idles (lint, test, coverage with codecov), in the tree and in the pre-commit guard, which reads the staged declaration. The same step removed or the same job idled without the declaration is still refused, and a constant false `if` that ends `&& false` is now recognised as one. Spec: `gate-declaration.spec.mts`.
- Fixed (`app sync` CI render): with build off, the database step of the lite build job builds the root packages itself (`npm run build:modules --silent && npm run db:lint ...`), since nothing before it does (`CI_STANDARD` packages-built-first); an app without fe renders no `build fe` step when build is off.
- Fixed (R343, PROJECT-BRANCH-1): the pre-push hook accepts the scaffold's first commit as it is. A root commit whose tree holds no cache file (`pristineFirstCommit`, `cache/freshness.mts`) pushes no record, so `HFS_CHECK_CACHE_UNWARMED` and its siblings refuse nothing for it; the bootstrap push of `main` and `dev` at that commit passes with hooks, CI runs it in full, and the cache the hook's run wrote is the first commit on `dev`. Spec: `check-cache-freshness.spec.mts`.
- Changed (pins, `app scaffold`): `tslib` 2.9.1 (was 2.8.1) and a root override at the pin. `@whatwg-node/promise-helpers` 2 (under `@graphql-tools/utils` 12.1.0) requires `^2.9.1`, while `@nestjs` 11 declares exactly 2.8.1 and `@graphql-codegen/typescript` `~2.8.0`, so no range meets every dependency of the lite and full starters and a fresh scaffold was red on `HFS_DEP_VERSION_SKEW`; the override keeps one copy. Upgrade: set `tslib` to 2.9.1 in the root `dependencies` and `overrides` of package.json and run `npm install`.

## 6.13.1 - 2026-11-15

- Fixed (lite managed passport template, slots `be.integrations.passport` and `be.identity.sign-in`): the passport error family names the three API-key codes `@starci/auth` 0.7.0 exports: `PASSPORT_API_KEY_REJECTED` (kind unauthenticated, HTTP 401), `PASSPORT_API_KEY_MALFORMED` (invalid, 400) and `PASSPORT_API_KEY_RECORD_INVALID` (internal, 500), each with the kind `AUTH_ERROR_KINDS` gives it (the kit spec holds the parity), and `IdentitySignInService` reads them as passport codes. The status comes from the kind through the one status table; the reason of a rejected key stays in the log and no code carries it on the wire. An app that holds the parity spec of the package's `PASSPORT_*` codes no longer sees the three as unused. Upgrade, per existing lite app: `starci app sync --execute` renders the two files. Specs `templates/be/skeleton-lite/src/modules/integrations/passport/passport.kit.spec.ts`, `templates/be/auth-lite/src/modules/domain/identity/sign-in.service.spec.ts`.
- Changed: the pins name `@starci/auth` 0.7.0 (the API-key strategy) and `@starci/ai-router` 0.7.0 (the Anthropic Messages route beside OpenAI, LangChain clients by name, prompt-cache breakpoints), and `@langchain/core` 1.3.5 and `@langchain/openai` 1.8.2, the floors of the optional peer ranges ai-router 0.7.0 declares, so an app that installs the pinned set resolves without ERESOLVE. Upgrade: an app raises the four in its package.json and lockfile; `starci app check` names the drift (`HFS_CANON_PIN_DRIFT`) until it does.

## 6.13.0 - 2026-11-14

- Added (PROJECT-LINT-4, R430 `gate-declaration`, schema, `app check`, hooks, release gate, `app sync` CI): an app declares gates off for the whole app and every branch in `gates` of `application-stacks.yaml` (`{ run: off, reason }`, the reason mandatory): any gate but `hfs`, `secrets` and `db`, the harness's own rules, which are never switchable. The ids are lint, format, sonar, codecov, test, coverage (the per-file threshold), types, build, audit, wiring, checks (the project's ext checks) and deps (`starci deps check`); a gate whose reader stays on (test: coverage, codecov, sonar; lint: sonar; coverage: codecov) is refused, and so is an unknown id, a missing reason or a never-switchable gate, as `HFS_GATE_DECLARATION_INVALID`. One reader (`readGateDeclarations`, folded by `activeProfile`) serves `app check` in both envs, the pre-commit hook (lint, format, the unit-spec guard) and the pre-push hook (`deps check`, except on a push of main or a release tag), the release gate (drops the rows of the gate and names them in its plan and the L4 record; the build rows always run) and the rendered CI (an echo line `off (declared: <reason>)` in place of the step, a skipped job the verdict expects, no Sonar job), and each prints `<gate>: off (declared: <reason>)`, never counting it as passed; `app check --profile` lists the reason. The gate ids `build`, `audit`, `checks`, `coverage` and `deps` are also `--profile --gate` words. `policy/loosening.yaml` gains `optionalGates`.
- Added (PROJECT-STACKS-5, R429 `secret-targets`, project-ext manifest): `starci app secret set|unset|list --env <env> --ext <target>` seals into a secret target the project's `ext/manifest.json` declares in `secretTargets` (`{id, why, root, environments: {<env>: <file>}, recipients, under?, depth?}`) instead of a member of the stack: the target resolves through the extension, the prompt, stdin, `--from-file`, `--generate` and the sops seam are the member's, the seam is rooted at the target's checkout so its own `.sops.yaml` names the recipients, a nested file is addressed by `/`-joined keys (`depth`, below `under`), and an unknown target, an environment the target does not seal, a missing checkout or `.sops.yaml`, a key of another depth or one carrying an environment is refused (`SECRET_TARGET_*`). R429 judges a declared file when its checkout is on the machine: absent `HFS_SECRET_TARGET_MISSING`, no sops block `HFS_SECRET_TARGET_UNSEALED`, a non-ENC value `HFS_SECRET_TARGET_PLAIN`. The manifest key is optional, so the extension API stays 1.
- Added (PROJECT-STACKS-15, R428 `stacks-storage-public`, schema, `stack up|verify|doctor`): an object store a dev environment runs has a public route on the dev tunnel. `local.services[]` takes `kind: object-store` and `console`; `local.tunnel` `{name, routes: [{name, host, port}]}` declares the public hosts of the machine's locally managed named tunnel. `stack up --execute` adds each missing host to the tunnel's ingress file (`<cloudflared home>/<name>.yml`, text-edited before the catch-all, a host routed elsewhere is `STACK_LOCAL_TUNNEL_CONFLICT`) and makes the DNS record with `cloudflared --config <that file> tunnel route dns --overwrite-dns <tunnel id> <host>` over `cert.pem`, the id read from the file's `tunnel:` (never the home's default `config.yml`, whose tunnel would win) (`STACK_LOCAL_TUNNEL_CERT`; `STACK_LOCAL_TUNNEL_CONFIG`, also for a file with no `tunnel:`; `STACK_LOCAL_TUNNEL_DNS`, also when cloudflared reports the record routed to another tunnel id), prints the public origins and reads `https://<host>/minio/health/live`; `stack verify` fails when that read is not 200; `stack doctor` has the `cloudflared`, certificate and ingress-file rows. R428 (`STORAGE_PUBLIC_ROUTE_MISSING`, `STORAGE_PUBLIC_ROUTE_CONSOLE`, `STORAGE_PUBLIC_LINK_INTERNAL`) judges a public-facing dev environment: its object store has a route to its S3 port (never its console) and every process that sets `STORAGE_ENDPOINT` sets `STORAGE_PUBLIC_URL` to the https host of that route (the `publicEndpoint` option of `@starci/object-storage`). An environment that publishes nothing is not judged; production is not judged. The starters declare no tunnel and are unchanged.
- Changed: R426 `DATA_STORE_ADAPTER` accepts a store module that holds a class implementing `StoreAdapter` or a factory whose declared return type is `StoreAdapter` (a package store such as `postgresStore` of `@starci/seed/postgres`).
- Added: a dev process that lists `DATABASE_OWNER_URL` in its `files` gets the environment's owner connection as `DATABASE_OWNER_URL_FILE` (written by `stack up` from custody, never sealed, never handed to another run): the whole-database store of a seed-backup reads past row level security and restores with `session_replication_role`, which the app role may not. Spec: `stack-local.spec.mts`.
- Added (PROJECT-STACKS-13, closes its Known gap): `starci app data status|verify|sync --env <env>` (`data/`): the explicit read of a seed-backup copy through the git driver of `@starci/seed` (a new exact dependency, 0.1.0) over a checkout under the harness state base, with the harness's one age identity lookup. `status` lists per backup set the newest copies (takenAt, export digest, items per store, sealed bytes against the 500 MiB cap); `verify` is the driver's full verify; `sync --set <id> --from <commit>` is a dry run that prints created, updated, unchanged and removed per store and set, and `--execute` applies it with mode `merge` (no mirror sync yet). Refusals: `DATA_SYNC_PURPOSE`, `DATA_SYNC_OTHER_ENVIRONMENT`, `DATA_SYNC_SNAPSHOT_MISSING`, `DATA_SYNC_COPY_UNHEALTHY`, `DATA_SYNC_TARGET_UNPROTECTED` (the reset rule: a healthy copy of this environment younger than `maxAgeMinutes`, default 1440, other than the snapshot), `DATA_SYNC_UNREACHABLE` (only a dev docker-compose environment's stores are reached from here), `DATA_IDENTITY_MISSING`, `DATA_COPY_UNREACHABLE`, `DATA_USAGE`. Spec `src/data-verb.spec.mts`.
- Added (R426, R427, schema): `environments.<env>.short` (lowercase kebab, at most 12 characters, unique) is the `<env>` of `<name>-seed-backup-<env>` and what `{env}` becomes, else the environment's name (`DATA_COPY_NAME` also judges the short name); a store's `adapter.command` is the argv of the app command that serves it over `starci/data-entries@1`, required for a store of a seed-backup set (`DATA_STORE_ADAPTER`); a seed-backup's `maxAgeMinutes` (a standard may not carry it, `DATA_FORCE_MISPLACED`).
- Fixed: `stack up` ran a declared seed command once per command digest only, so a stack brought up on new volumes answered "already done" from a state file that outlived the data; the receipt now also holds the compose project's volume names and creation times, and new volumes run the seed commands again (PROJECT-STACKS-6). Spec: `stack-local.spec.mts`.
- Changed (R400, breaking for a project that used the `<project>-data@<major>` format): data lives in git only in a seed copy repository, `<name>-seed-standard` (plain, pinned by commit, read once by the bring-up) or `<name>-seed-backup-<env>` (one environment, always sealed, read only by an explicit sync): a copy repository has no hfs.json and a root manifest.json of schema `starci/data-manifest@1` (PROJECT-STACKS-13). In a code project `HFS_DATA_IN_CODE` refuses a tracked copy manifest, the data/ blobs/ index/ tree next to one and a sops binary-mode envelope, and `HFS_DATA_DEPENDENCY` a package dependency, workspace, submodule or tsconfig path naming a repository that ends `-seed-standard` or `-seed-backup-<env>`. `data-repository.mts` is `data-copies.mts`; `STACKS_DATA_REPOSITORY` and the earlier format are gone. Spec `src/data-copies.spec.mts`.
- Added (R400, the commit hook's copy repository guard in `sync/hygiene.mts`): in a copy repository the staged index is judged for `HFS_DATA_UNSEALED` (a seed-backup file that is no `*.sops.json` sops envelope), `HFS_DATA_STANDARD_SEALED` (a sops envelope or `*.sops.json` / `*.enc.yaml` file in a seed-standard, or a manifest that says sealing), `HFS_DATA_MANIFEST_DRIFT` (the manifest `files` against the tracked data files, the purpose against the format name), `HFS_DATA_FILE_SIZE` (a data file over 4 MiB) and `HFS_DATA_CAP` (the data files over 500 MiB: the data set needs the s3 driver).
- Added (R426, `data-stores`): every stateful component or declared backing is the `component` of a store or of a `derived` entry of the `data:` declaration of `application-stacks.yaml`; a store has a kind of the closed list, a level and an `adapter.module` that is a tracked source file holding a class that `implements StoreAdapter`; a derived entry names a declared store and how it is rebuilt (`DATA_STORE_UNDECLARED`, `DATA_STORE_KIND`, `DATA_STORE_ADAPTER`, `DATA_DERIVED_SOURCE`). The stateful definition of R267 moved to `stateful-components.mts`, one definition for both rules; R267 is unchanged. Spec `src/data-stores.spec.mts`.
- Added (R427, `data-sets`): each `data.sets[]` entry has a purpose, declared stores and environments, a driver of the closed list and a copy name of the naming law; a standard is pinned by a 40-hex commit, `force`/`commit` belong to a standard and `everyMinutes` to a backup, `completeness` names a check of ext/manifest.json, and no seeds.yaml entry of any level loads a backup (`DATA_PURPOSE`, `DATA_DRIVER_KIND`, `DATA_COPY_NAME`, `DATA_SEED_UNPINNED`, `DATA_FORCE_MISPLACED`, `DATA_BACKUP_LOADED`, `DATA_EXTENSION_UNDECLARED`). Spec `src/data-sets.spec.mts`.
- Changed: the `data` member of `application-stacks.schema.yaml` is the new declaration (stores, derived, sets). The Grammar family and the two canons are cut with this release (CONTRIB-20): `@starci/eslint-canon-be` 3.27.0, `@starci/eslint-canon-fe` 9.20.0, `@starci/grammar` and its two lint factories 0.39.3. The shared constants of `@starci/common` data/words.ts (4 MiB, 500 MiB, the closed lists) are copied into the checkers: @starci/hfs does not depend on @starci/common.
- Fixed (R426): a fresh starter passes its own check. `derived.from` is optional (a source outside the project, such as an ingress's ACME certificates, names none; a `from` that is present still names a declared store), and both starters declare a `data:` block: the database as a store whose adapter is the new skeleton file `be/src/modules/integrations/seed/database.adapter.ts` (`DatabaseStore implements StoreAdapter`, no set yet; the app pins `@starci/seed` 0.1.0, which canon-pins.yaml now lists) and the ingress as `derived` with its rebuild.
- Added (slot `be.integrations.seed`, manifest 3.40.0; R90 `ruleParams.be.infraOwners`; BE-INFRA-1, PROJECT-STACKS-14): `@starci/seed` is imported only by its one integration, `src/modules/integrations/seed/` (requires `index.ts`, `seed.options.ts`, `seed.decorators.ts`, `seed.module-definition.ts`, `seed.module.ts`; `"@starci/seed": [integrations/seed]`), and every store adapter is registered by a module composed into an app, so the file a store's `adapter.module` names is reached from the app's root, not named by the stack declaration alone: an adapter that reads only through platform is `integrations/seed/<store>.adapter.ts`, registered by `DataSeedModule`; one that reads a domain's data lives in a domain capability, registered by its module, and takes the port from `@modules/integrations/seed` (BE-INFRA-3). The starter's `DatabaseStore` moved there from `platform/data-stores/` (which no longer exists) and passes the canon it ships with: composed by `DataSeedModule` (the full cli app's `compose/integrations.ts`, the lite api app's `integrationModules`), its `identityOf` answers the named `StoreIdentity` of `seed.contracts.ts`, its empty `export` returns a done async iterable instead of an `async` generator with no `await`, and a unit spec covers it. An app that kept an adapter under `platform/data-stores/` moves it as above (platform may not import the package), registers it in `DataSeedModule` and points `data.stores[].adapter.module` at the new path. Specs `src/scaffold-template-canon-be.spec.mts` (the integration is linted in both editions), `src/scaffold-type-check.spec.mts` (links the sibling `@starci/seed` like the other packages a starter compiles against; it dropped it before, so a fresh app never found the package), `src/data-stores.spec.mts`.

## 6.12.0 - 2026-11-14

- A minor cut on 6.10.0: 6.11.0 is held by the open branch `pkg/claude-lint-grammar-split`, so this release is 6.12.0. It carries the Grammar family 0.39.1 of `pkg/claude-grammar-flow-map` (FlowGraph steps) and `@starci/eslint-canon-be` 3.25.2 (health-signals fix) under its own cut.
- Changed (BE-JOBS-7, BE-RESILIENCE-2, R377): a failure of a job ends on one ladder per class of flow and then as a dead letter with one item for a person per park episode; a new build hands back every class, the periodic sweep only transient dead letters a bounded number of times without a new item, a person's resume opens a new episode; a delivery started past its ladder is a dead letter without running; scheduled jobs and an event consumer's buried event are judged by the same classifier. R377 `BE_FLOW_FAILURE_SWALLOWED` also judges the catch blocks of a `modules/domain` file that names `ClaimedJob` (the work a job calls): feature and domain code never catches to wait, loop, retry, count or swallow. Law text, messages and the failure code's meaning say dead letter. Spec `src/hfs-flow-failure.spec.mts`.
- Changed (BE-SAGA-1, R169 `BE_SAGA_STEP_COMPENSATION`, breaking for a saga that relied on the finding): a flow moves forward only. A compensation is the optional declared undo of a step, pressed by a person and run as an ordinary forward step; a step with no compensation is no finding, a compensation with no step and an orchestrator that omits a step or a compensation still are. BE-SAGA-5 and BE-SAGA-6 are unchanged; BE-SAGA-7 names the `waited` ladder (R392 stays planned). Specs `src/hfs-saga-rules.spec.mts`, `src/hfs-saga-coverage.spec.mts`.
- Changed (BE-JOBS-3, R159, breaking for an app that passes `RunKey`): `BE_JOB_RUN_KEY` is `BE_JOB_EFFECT_PIECE`. An outside WRITE of a job goes through the effects piece: in a step, and in a function that takes a `ClaimedJob`, every call on an integration port is the `act` or the `confirm` read of an `Effects.once` call of `platform/effects`, unless its port declares the member an outside READ (`OutsideReads<"member">` of `platform/effects`), which needs no intent. The `@starci/eslint-canon-be` rule is `job-effect-piece` (3.26.0).
- Added (BE-JOBS-9, BE-JOBS-10, R403 `BE_JOB_NEXT_DECLARED`, R404 `BE_JOB_PROGRESS_SHAPE`): a job declares itself with `defineJob` and starts, and enqueues its next job, only from an outbox row written in the transaction of the change that calls for it; its progress is an optional view of the job rows, served by one generic door. The two failure codes, their catalog entries and their slot rules are in the catalogs; `@starci/eslint-canon-be` 3.26.0 holds the checks.
- Changed (BE-REALTIME-2, BE-REALTIME-8, R185, R186): an SSE door may call ONE stream read service (a class of the platform or domain tier whose public methods only answer an `Observable`) instead of the hub, with the principal, so a stream can begin with the current state while the door stays one call (`feature-thin` accepts it). Catalog laws and the failure code's meaning say so.
- Changed (R152): `BE_OUTBOX_WRITE_TX` also judges a direct `QueueOutbox.write`: a domain service, inside its own transaction.
- Changed: the knowledge of the patterns (`jobs.yaml`, `queues.yaml`, `saga.yaml`, `realtime.yaml`) and `contexts/code/be/{jobs,resilience,queues,saga,realtime,event-bus}.md` teach the chain of jobs, the ladders, the dead letter, the effects piece, forward-only flows and the progress door. Known gap: the `platform/jobs` templates still render the claim, the fence, the sweep and the resumer; the ladders, `defineJob`, `platform/effects`, the episodes and the progress view are an app's own platform code until a template carries them.
- Changed: the pins name `@starci/eslint-canon-be` 3.26.0, `@starci/eslint-canon-fe` 9.19.0 and the Grammar family 0.39.2 (the 0.39.1 FlowGraph steps content plus this cut). Upgrade, per existing app: a step or a domain method that calls an integration port for a job makes the call the `act` of `Effects.once`; a saga that listed a compensation for every step keeps them (each is now a declared undo).

## 6.10.0 - 2026-11-13

- Changed: the core grammar's playbook names its dashboard recipe `peer-sections-dashboard` (it was named after one app's audience) and its conversation categories All, Groups, Contacts and Unread.
- Changed (PROJECT-LINT-3, R382; breaking for an app that relied on the fixed layout): the harness knows no folder name of a root package. The packages view carries `packageLint` (`packageLintScopes` of `runtime/scripts/hfs/package-slots.mts`): the slots the project declares `lint: "fe"`, first slot wins, which the canons' packages configs read. Coverage has no default folder either: `PACKAGE_UNMEASURED` (`src/workbench/**`, `src/public/**`, `src/client/**`) is gone, so a package the project does not declare is measured whole but its barrels and type modules, and a declared one as its slots say. `canonScope` reads a `files` entry that is a list of globs as all of them. Upgrade, per existing app: declare the front-end folders of each package as `lint: "fe"` slots with `coverage: { none: "<reason>" }` (`ext/manifest.json` `packages`, or the package's `slots.yaml` while that read path lasts). Specs `src/package-slots.spec.mts`, `src/lint-canon-scope.spec.mts`, `src/hfs-coverage-below.spec.mts`.
- Changed: the harness tree holds no app, product or provider identifier (contexts/harness/rules.md rule 9): spec fixtures, knowledge texts, comments and messages of this package use neutral names; no rule id, finding code or behaviour changes with them.
- Removed: the agent-runtime layer (`stack/runtime-layers.mts`, the layer recipe under `stack/images/`, its step of `starci app stack up`, the layer copy of `scripts/sync-bundles.mts`) and the fork entry of R374's `fork-images.yaml` that served it; the fork list keeps the object store. The three unused product-name constants of `runtime/scripts/lib/example-refs.mts` are gone.
- Changed (R252): the home secret folder `HFS_SCRIPT_PLAINTEXT_LOAD` guards is `.<project>/`, the project name of the app's hfs.json, read per run (`runtime/scripts/hfs/rules/script-hygiene.mts`); it was one app's folder name. Spec `src/hfs-script-hygiene.spec.mts`.
- Changed (PROJECT-WORK-7, R212): slot `app.starciwork` admits the Markdown concepts `concepts/README.md` and `concepts/<topic>.md` in both editions, and the starter of both carries the catalog and one example topic; the YAML records `concepts/index.yaml` and `concepts/<name>/index.yaml` an app still keeps stay admitted. No gate reads a concept. Spec `src/hfs-starciwork-lite-forbidden.spec.mts`.
- Removed (breaking for an app that used them): the module scaffold. The verbs `starci app add module <name>` and `starci app remove module <name>` (the whole `remove` verb, `scaffold/add-module.mts`, `templates/app/module/**`), the registry package `packages/modules` that `starci app sync` rendered (`scaffold/module-registry.mts`), the slot `app.module-registry` of both slot manifests (manifest 3.39.0) and the hfs.json key `modules` (`"data"`) are gone. A root workspace package is any `packages/<name>/` holding a `package.json` and a `src/` (slot `app.packages`, `AppSourceFacts.rootPackages`); `packages/modules` is one like any other, measured and linted as the app's own code, and the managed `build:modules` script still builds them all. Upgrade: such an app deletes the `modules` key from its hfs.json (an hfs.json that keeps it is refused as an unknown key, `HFS_DECLARATION_INVALID`) and owns its `packages/modules` and module packages itself. Specs `src/architecture-root-packages.spec.mts`, `src/hfs-lexicon.spec.mts`.
- Changed: the pins name `@starci/test-world` 2.0.0, `@starci/heroicons` 0.4.0, `@starci/jest-preset` 2.9.1 and `@starci/ai-router` 0.6.1.
- Added (R263): hfs.json `sides.be.receiptPorts`, a list of `{ class, method, repeat, reason }` (`repeat` is `"false"` or `"undefined"`, each class and method once, be side only; any other shape is `HFS_DECLARATION_INVALID`, `receiptPortsOk` of `runtime/scripts/hfs/rule-params-shape.mts`), handed to the back-end canon as `ruleParams.receiptPorts`. `@starci/eslint-canon-be` 3.25.0 reads it in `webhook-inbox-key`. Spec `src/hfs-database-access.spec.mts`.
- Added (R263): a `sides.be.receiptPorts` entry may be `{ class, method, many: true, reason }` for a delivery that carries several events, whose method answers the list of the new events (the empty list on a repeat); an entry gives `repeat` or `many: true`, never both, and `many` is only `true` (`receiptPortsOk`, the `ReceiptPort` type of `runtime/scripts/hfs/manifest-shape.mts`). Spec `src/hfs-database-access.spec.mts`.
- Changed (R12, `HFS_E2E_IN_AUTOMATIC_GATE`, manifest 3.39.0): the integration, e2e and contract specs run on a local machine only, by hand (`npm run test:e2e`) and as the world rows of the release gate; no CI workflow runs them, whatever its trigger. `starci app sync` renders no `.github/workflows/e2e.yml` (the template is gone) and `starci app sync --execute` removes a tracked one in either edition (`RETIRED_FILES`, `sync/index.mts`); slot `app.ci-e2e` is forbidden in both editions (`HFS_FORBIDDEN_PRESENT` names the local run), and the detector judges every workflow, a `workflow_dispatch`, `workflow_call` or scheduled one included (`runtime/scripts/hfs/architecture/automatic-gates.mts`). The release gate keeps its `test:integration`, `test:e2e` and `test:contract` rows, and `starci app check` runs none of them, as before. Specs `src/hfs-e2e-automatic-gate.spec.mts`, `src/ci-pipeline.spec.mts`, `src/hfs-slot-ownership.spec.mts`.
- Upgrade, per existing full app: run `starci app sync --execute`, which deletes `.github/workflows/e2e.yml`, and commit the deletion; until then `starci app check` is red on it (`HFS_FORBIDDEN_PRESENT`, `HFS_E2E_IN_AUTOMATIC_GATE`). A workflow of the app's own that runs `test:integration`, `test:e2e` or `test:contract` loses that step.

## 6.9.0 - 2026-11-13

- Added (PROJECT-LINT-3, R381): the project extension folder. A project adds its own laws in `ext/` at the app root (slot `app.ext`, manifest 3.38.0), one subfolder per tool, all declared in `ext/manifest.json` (`starci/project-ext@1`, with `api`, the extension API version it targets): `ext/hfs/` holds machine rules (`(context) => findings`, run by `starci app check` over the tracked files the rule's `appliesTo` names), `ext/lint/` ESLint rules the canon factories load over those paths, `ext/checks/` the project checks (R402, below). `starci app check` loads and runs them on every run. A declared file that is not there, a rule file no rule declares, a rule that throws, any key that would turn off, override or lower a rule, and a code the harness reports refuse the extension (`HFS_EXT_INVALID`), which then lends nothing while the check is red; a manifest of another API version is refused `HFS_EXT_API_MISMATCH`, naming the project to update. `runtime/scripts/lib/project-ext.mts`; spec `src/project-ext.spec.mts`.
- Changed (R16): `HFS_TOOL_CONFIG_LOCAL` does not judge a lint rule file of `ext/lint/` that `ext/manifest.json` declares (`declaredLintFiles`, `sync/managed.mts`); the same file undeclared, and a rule file anywhere else, stay red. Spec `src/hfs-tool-config-local.spec.mts`.
- Changed: a finding under an extension rule's own code is reported with the rule's id and doc (`extensionWhy`, read by `readWhy` of `runtime/scripts/hfs/check.mts`); a code that neither the failure-code catalog nor a loaded extension declares is still refused `HFS_MANIFEST_INVALID`.
- Fixed (R163, R397): `HFS_SERVICE_PLACEMENT` no longer refuses the `be/apps/<name>/package.json` that `BE_CLI_CLIENT` requires of an app hfs.json declares with kind `client-cli`; the two rules contradicted each other on 6.8.6. Every other non-workspace `package.json` (another be app's, an undeclared folder's, a nested one) stays a second service root (`runtime/scripts/hfs/rules/services.mts`). Spec `src/hfs-services-legacy-coverage.spec.mts`.
- Fixed (R248): `ENV_SEALED_UNREAD` counts the dev stack's own database declaration as a consumer: the `{ member, key }` of `environments.<env>.local.database.password` and `.adminPassword`, which `starci app stack up` reads to create the owner role and write the database URL (`localDatabaseKeys`, `runtime/scripts/hfs/rules/env-parity.mts`). A `local.custody` entry is no consumer: a key listed only there is still unread. Spec `src/hfs-env-parity.spec.mts`.
- Added (R248): `ENV_KEY_ENVIRONMENT_NAMED`. A sealed or declared key whose name starts with an environment token (`DEV_`, `PROD_`, `PRODUCTION_`, `STAGING_`, `LOCAL_`, `TEST_`, an exact leading token) is an error naming the bare key: the environment of a key is its folder, `.starcistacks/<env>/secrets/`, so one name serves every environment and each seals its own value (`runtime/scripts/hfs/rules/env-parity.mts`). The lite starter's `DEV_DB_OWNER_PASSWORD` and `DEV_DB_APP_PASSWORD` are `DB_OWNER_PASSWORD` and `DB_APP_PASSWORD` (application-stacks.yaml `local.database`, `local.custody`, the dev `KEYS.md`); the old two names are a warning for this minor and an error from the next. `starci app sync` prints, per old name, the files to rename it in (`environmentKeyNotices`, `sync/harness-checks.mts`) and renames nothing. Spec `src/hfs-env-parity.spec.mts`, `src/project-checks.spec.mts`.
- Upgrade, per existing lite app: rename `DEV_DB_OWNER_PASSWORD` to `DB_OWNER_PASSWORD` and `DEV_DB_APP_PASSWORD` to `DB_APP_PASSWORD` in `.starcistacks/application-stacks.yaml` and `.starcistacks/<env>/runtime/env/KEYS.md`, then the owner seals each value under the new name (`starci app secret init --env <env> --execute`, or `rotate`), in the same change; any other key that starts with an environment name is renamed the same way and is red at once.
- Removed: rule packs. hfs.json `rulePacks`, the `starci/rule-pack@1` manifest of an installed package, `runtime/scripts/lib/rule-packs.mts` and `HFS_RULE_PACK_INVALID` are gone; the declaration check names `ext/` for a leftover `rulePacks` or `checks` key of hfs.json. No app declared one, and no release carried hfs.json `checks` or `scripts/checks/`.
- Changed (R382): a root package's slots are declared by the project, in `ext/manifest.json` under `packages["packages/<name>"]` (`closed`, `slots`), and a slot may name the canon that judges it (`lint: be | fe`, read by `frontEndGlobs` of `package-slots.mts`). `packages/<name>/slots.yaml` is still read for a package the manifest does not declare, with the warning `HFS_PACKAGE_SLOTS_MOVED`, for this minor only; a package declared in both is `HFS_PACKAGE_SLOTS_INVALID`.
- Extension API 1: the manifest keys `schema`, `api`, `rules`, `checks`, `packages`; a rule's keys `id`, `code`, `severity`, `doc`, `appliesTo` and one of `hfs`, `lint` (`{ tool: "eslint", file }`); a check's `{ id, run, why }`; an hfs rule's context `{ api, repoRoot, files, read }` and finding `{ path, message, line?, column? }`. A breaking change to any of these raises the number.
- Upgrade, per existing app: an app with `packages/<name>/slots.yaml` moves each file's `closed` and `slots` into `ext/manifest.json` (`{ "schema": "starci/project-ext@1", "api": 1, "packages": { "packages/<name>": { ... } } }`) and deletes the file, before the next minor. Needs `@starci/eslint-canon-be` 3.24.0 and `@starci/eslint-canon-fe` 9.17.0, which read `project-ext.mjs`.
- Fixed: the package's own spec run (`npm test`) starts as many spec processes at once as the heavy governor admitted it with. `scripts/node-test.mts` runs `node --test` with `--test-concurrency` of `STARCI_TEST_WORKERS` and each spec process under `STARCI_WORKER_HEAP_MB`, both set by the harness CLI's test-run sizing; unbounded, `node --test` started the cores less one at once (27 on a 28-core host, 13.4 GB of commit against the 6.7 GB the same suite takes at 6), outgrew the plan its slot was admitted on, and on a busy host a spec's `git` or `sh` child failed to commit memory (exit 3221225773, STATUS_COMMITMENT_LIMIT, or an MSYS fork failure), turning an unrelated spec red (`repo-identity`, `stack-ssh-gate` identity-restore and release store). With neither variable set (CI, a plain run) node's defaults hold.
- Added (R389, PROJECT-STACKS-10): the base port overlap with the machine's other registered projects, so far the doctor's `port range` row and the scaffold's, is judged by two more verbs from the same projects (`stack/local-peers.mts` `baseOverlap`, `overlapLine`, `peersOf`; the doctor row reads them too). `starci app stack up --env <env>`, dry run and `--execute`, refuses `STACK_LOCAL_BASE_PORT_TAKEN` before anything starts, naming the other project, everything compared and the next free base. `starci app check --env local` reports `STACK_BASE_PORT_OVERLAP`, a warning (report-only), with one `machine <env>: base-port-overlap` line per dev environment that declares a base (`stack/local-live-checks.mts`): the tree may be lawful and either project may move, CI has no machine registry (`--env prod` never asks), and stack up is where it blocks. Specs: `src/stack-base-port.spec.mts` (a temp machine store under `STARCI_LOCAL_ROOT`: overlap reported and free range one line; up refused dry and executed with no command started).
- Added (R402, PROJECT-LINT-3): project checks. `ext/manifest.json` `checks: [{ id, run, why }]` declares each (a unique kebab-case id, `run` a unique `ext/checks/<name>.mjs`, the reason; any other shape, or a declared file that is not there, is `HFS_EXT_INVALID`), and `starci app check` runs every declared check as its own step, `checks` (`cache/project-checks.mts`, `cache/schedule.mts`), wherever the hfs gate runs: with this Node, from the app root, a `build` child of the heavy governor, never cached. Exit 0 is green; any other exit, or a missing file, is `HFS_PROJECT_CHECK_FAILED` with the check, its exit, what it guards and the last 20 lines it printed. Spec `src/project-checks.spec.mts`.
- Changed (R103): `HFS_REPO_LOCAL_CHECK` judges a check by the word, not a `check-` prefix: a runnable file (`.js`, `.mjs`, `.cjs`, `.ts`, `.mts`, `.cts`, `.sh`, `.ps1`) of `scripts/` or `tools/` (or one folder below) whose name holds check, checks or checker as a word split on `-`, `_` and `.` (`tool-names-check.mjs`, `flow_check.cjs`, `ids.check.mjs`, `checks-all.sh`, `seed-checker.ts`; never `checkout.mjs`); a file of `ext/checks/` that `ext/manifest.json` `checks` does not declare; and a package script that runs either (one that runs a declared check stays). `starci app sync` names each loose check-named script with the move to `ext/checks/`, the manifest entry to add and the package scripts to repoint, and never moves it.
- Upgrade, per existing app: an app whose `scripts/` or `tools/` holds a check-named file (`*-check.mjs` and the forms above) turns red on R103; run `starci app sync` for the notice, move the file to `ext/checks/`, declare it in `ext/manifest.json` `checks`, repoint its package script.

App kind and trigger kind `client-cli`; slots `be.app.client-cli` (`be/apps/<app>/src/`), `be.app.client-cli.package` (`be/apps/<app>/{package.json,tsconfig.build.json,README.md}`) and `be.client-cli` (`be/src/features/client-cli/<app>/`, composed only by an app of kind `client-cli`). An app entry of `hfs.json` may carry `package` and `bin`.

R398 `BE_CLI_CLIENT_BOUNDARY` is `@starci/eslint-canon-be` 3.23.3 `cli-client-boundary`.

- Changed: slot field `exceptAppKinds` (the app kinds whose `<app>` folders a slot neither owns nor requires): `repo.app-image` excepts `client-cli`, so a Dockerfile under a client cli is owned by no slot. An app kind has exactly one slot without a `parent`.
- Changed: `ruleParams.be.unitRoles[].slots` is a list (was `slot`): the `cli` role is unit-tested in `be.cli` and `be.client-cli`. Needs `@starci/eslint-canon-be` 3.23.3.

To add one: declare it in `hfs.json` (`sides.be.apps` and `client-cli` in `sides.be.kinds`), write `be/apps/<app>/` and `be/src/features/client-cli/<app>/`, then `starci app sync --execute`. Publishing the package (`npm publish be/apps/<app>` after `npm run build:<app>`) is the app's own step: the harness publishes no app package.
