## 6.8.3 - 2026-11-12

- Added (R401, BE-INJECTION-5): `BE_DI_RING`. The lite boot proof (R272, `runtime/scripts/hfs/app-wiring.mts`) registers the real AppModule, with the fixture's options, with Nest's own `DependenciesScanner` before it compiles (it instantiates nothing) and walks the recorded provider graph (`runtime/scripts/hfs/di-ring.mts`): constructor parameters (`@Inject` included), `@Inject` properties, `useFactory` `inject` entries and `useExisting` targets, each resolved with Nest's lookup (own providers, then imports through re-exports; globals bound to every module); an `@Optional` dependency is an edge when it resolves; a `forwardRef` one is none (Nest 11 defers it and resolves the ring it closes at any length, through a class, an alias or a factory; R85 `no-forward-ref` refuses it). Every elementary ring of two or more providers is one error finding, `A -> B -> C -> A` with each provider's module, source file and edge, and the compile is then skipped: Nest refuses a ring of two (CircularDependencyException) and waits forever on a longer one, so a three-provider ring through a `useExisting` port hung the app at boot with no error. The check runs wherever the boot proof runs: the wiring step of `starci app check` and `starci app check --gate wiring` (the lite CI fast job); full keeps its e2e boot. Specs: `src/hfs-di-ring.spec.mts` (the three-provider `useExisting` ring named; the same app with the port on its own provider green; a ring of two and a ring of factories named; a ring closed by forwardRef passing; every elementary ring of a tangle once; an optional edge only when it resolves; forged worker text dropped); the factory-ring case moves there from `src/hfs-boot-proof.spec.mts`.
- Changed (R103): `starci app sync` retires the lane-local copy of the ring check, `scripts/di-ring-check.mjs`, in a lite app (the dry run names it, `--execute` deletes it) and drops every root package.json script that runs it, keeping the rest of package.json (`sync/harness-checks.mts`). Spec: `src/sync-harness-checks.spec.mts`.
- Upgrade, per existing lite app: run `starci app sync --execute` when the app kept `scripts/di-ring-check.mjs`; nothing else is written into an app.

## 6.8.2 - 2026-11-12

- Added: the dev stack of both starters runs an object store, the `storage` service: the StarCi source-built MinIO `ghcr.io/starci183/minio:RELEASE.2025-10-15T17-29-55Z-starci.1` pinned by digest (`minio` of contexts/stack/pins.yaml `images`, held equal to the harness `images/sources.json` by `src/dev-storage.spec.mts`), its root credentials the dev `storage` member's `STORAGE_ACCESS_KEY_ID` and `STORAGE_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` read through `*_FILE` from the least grant `STACK_GRANT_STORAGE`, its S3 API and console on loopback, waited on its health, a `storage-live` readiness probe and a `storage` console origin. The lite api's `STORAGE_ENDPOINT` is the service's port (`${port.storage}`, never the host's 9000); the full starter declares the member in its dev custody.
- Changed (BE-AUTH-8): the lite api's readiness waits for every required dependency, not only the database. `platform/http-security` (lite overlay) keeps the list, `RequiredProbesService` under `REQUIRED_PROBES` (`InjectRequiredProbes`, the `RequiredProbe` contract), and `ReadinessService.probeRequired` runs the database probe and every listed probe under the same one-second deadline, answering 503 when one fails. The object-storage integration adds `ObjectStorageProbeService` at boot (`onModuleInit`): its probe is `ensureBucket` of `@starci/object-storage`, a head that creates the bucket a fresh dev store lacks. Manifest 3.34.0: slot `be.integrations.object-storage` allows `object-storage-probe.service.ts`. Specs: `readiness.service.spec.ts` (lite), `required-probes.service.spec.ts`, `object-storage-probe.service.spec.ts`.
- Fixed: a starter without fe declares no `codegen` setup step in its dev `local` block (its script exists only with fe), so `starci app stack up` of a `--without fe` scaffold no longer fails on a missing script (`scaffold/sides.mts`; spec `src/scaffold-sides.spec.mts`).
- Changed (R389, PROJECT-STACKS-10): every dev host port derives from the base port. Native processes stay below base+80 (the further ones base+2 or base+3 to base+79); the compose services take base+80 to base+99, a port that plays a role on its slot: the port `local.database` names base+90, `mail` base+91, `mailui` base+92, `storage` base+93, `storage-console` base+94 (`COMPOSE_ROLES`, `composePort`, `runtime/scripts/hfs/stack-base-port.mts`). New failure codes: `STACKS_COMPOSE_PORT_ROLE` (a compose port of a role off its slot) and `STACKS_COMPOSE_PORT_LITERAL` (a compose file of an environment with a base publishes a literal host port, not `${STACK_PORT_<NAME>}`). The doctor's `base port` row judges both tables. The scaffold fills the derived ports (`scaffoldPorts`, `{{dbPort}}`, `{{mailPort}}`, `{{mailUiPort}}`, `{{storagePort}}`, `{{storageConsolePort}}`), so two new apps run side by side; the full starter's dev compose publishes its app ports on loopback from `local.ports`. Specs: `src/stack-base-port.spec.mts`, `src/dev-storage.spec.mts`, `src/mail-sink.spec.mts`.
- Fixed (R389, PROJECT-STACKS-10): each project owns its own hundred, and the base-port overlap check now finds the machine's projects. It compared none from an app's installed CLI, which keeps its own state base with no registry, so a base inside another project's hundred passed (`overlaps no other app of this machine (0 with a base port checked)`). `stack/local-peers.mts` reads the `ledgers` of every machine registry it sees: the state base's machine.sqlite and, unless STARCI_LOCAL_ROOT or the test registry pins one store, the harness home's `.runtime/machine.sqlite` (`harnessHomeOf`, `machineRegistries`); each project from its registered checkout and every worktree of it (`git worktree list`, lanes included); a project with no `basePort` holds the ports its `local.ports` declares; a checkout or registry it cannot read is named. The doctor's `port range` row names every project compared with its base or ports, the registries read and the unread checkouts, and fails with the next free base (`nextFreeBase`). `starci app scaffold` checks the same projects (`scaffoldBase`): without the new `--base-port <n>` it takes the name's base or the next free hundred of 3000..4900 and prints `base port: <n> (...)` naming why; an overlapping `--base-port` is refused `HFS_SCAFFOLD_BASE_PORT_TAKEN` with the next free base. Specs: `src/stack-base-port.spec.mts` (a fake machine store under a temp STARCI_LOCAL_ROOT with a lane worktree, a project with no base, a retired and a missing checkout), `src/stack-local.spec.mts`.
- Upgrade, per existing app: run `starci app sync --execute` once (the dry run names each edit as `HFS_SYNC_DRIFT` and each case it leaves as a `notice:` line; `sync/dev-services.mts`). For every docker-compose environment that declares `local.basePort` it moves the database, `mail` and `mailui` ports of `local.ports` to base+90, base+91 and base+92 (a slot another port already holds stays, named), and when the environment runs no object store (no `storage` port, no compose service of a MinIO image) and its `local.custody` declares the `storage` member's two keys, it adds the `storage` and `storage-console` ports, `{name: storage, wait: healthy}` to `services`, the `storage` grant of those keys, the `storage-live` probe to `ready`, the console to `origins`, points the api's `STORAGE_ENDPOINT` at `${port.storage}`, and appends the `storage` service and its `storage-data` volume to the environment's first compose file. Nothing else of either file changes: comments, order and every other environment stay. An environment without `basePort` is left as it is with a notice: declare the base first, then sync again. A compose file that publishes a literal host port (`STACKS_COMPOSE_PORT_LITERAL`) is the owner's edit: declare the port in `local.ports` and publish `127.0.0.1:${STACK_PORT_<NAME>}:<port>`.

## 6.8.1 - 2026-11-12

- Fixed (PROJECT-BRANCH-1): `starci app scaffold` leaves the new app on the branch work happens on: it stages every file it wrote, records them as the first commit of `main` and checks out `dev` at it (`settleScaffoldBranches`, `scaffold/app.mts`; git verbs `api/git/commit.mts`, `api/git/switch.mts`), so a whole `starci app check` no longer meets `HFS_DEV_BRANCH_MISSING` after the first commit and the first push sends `main` and `dev` together. A commit git refuses (no identity) leaves the files staged on unborn `main`; the output's `git:` line names what git said and the two steps left. The next steps name `npx starci app check`. Spec: `src/scaffold-standalone.spec.mts`.
- Changed: `starci app check` names what it does not judge: the overview counts the files git neither tracks nor ignores (`untracked: <n> files ... not judged`, `untrackedFiles` in `runtime/scripts/hfs/check.mts`, the run's own `.starcistacks/cache/` output aside) and says that `git add` includes them; the check still judges the index alone, the tree a commit carries and CI judges. `--json` carries `untracked`. Spec: `src/check-untracked.spec.mts`.
- Fixed (BE-TRANSPORT-4): `starci app add api <feature>` in an app whose api apps declare `contract: rest` writes the REST door, not a GraphQL one: `transport/http/<action>.controller.ts` (`@ApiOperation({ operationId: "<feature>.<action>" })`, `@ApiBearerAuth()`, `@ApiOkResponse`, `@ApiDefaultResponse({ type: ErrorResponse })`, the principal), `dto/<action>.request.ts` (bounded, `@ApiProperty`, JSDoc), `dto/<action>.response.ts` (the answer and the error body), the mapper and the HTTP module (`templates/be/patterns/api/http.*.tpl`), composes `<Feature>HttpModule.register({})` in the api group and opts into `be.contract.openapi`; no GraphQL peer, platform or slot. The api pattern's files tree marks an entry `contract: graphql|rest` (written for that contract alone, its `optional` notwithstanding) and `templates: {rest: ...}` (`entriesForContract`, `scaffold/add-plan.mts`). The verb's owner step names `npm run contract:emit`. Spec: `src/add-api-rest.spec.mts`.
- Fixed: `starci app add cli migrate` (lite) writes a cli that passes `starci app check`: the seed runner reads a seed as text (no import of the deferred `./database.sql`), the migration runner rejects with `DATABASE_MIGRATION_FAILED` (`status` param) of the database error family instead of a bare `Error` (`throw-domain-error`), the process it starts and the seed root are injected (`MIGRATION_COMMAND`, `SEED_ROOT` in `primary.decorators.ts`, provided by `DatabaseModule`), both runners get their unit specs (`migration-runner.service.spec.ts`, `seed-runner.service.spec.ts`, written by the add, never by the starter), the wiring proof doubles `PRIMARY_DATA_SOURCE` with no connection name leaving `platform/database` (`BE_CONNECTION_DUPLICATE`; the cli no longer exports `PRIMARY_CONNECTION`), the generated `run.cli.ts` and builder are Prettier-clean, and the managed targets the cli app changes (the images workflow, the CI shards) are rendered by the add. The owner step names `npm install`. Spec: `src/queues-cli-lite.spec.mts`.
- Fixed (R296): an unsealed service credential of a service that declares its open owner step (`ownerAction.needed`, the fresh scaffold's Sonar analysis token) is that step, not `CI_SECRET_SEALED`; once sealed, STACKS_OWNER_ACTION_REDUNDANT asks for `ownerAction: none`, so the declaration and the custody never disagree. A fresh scaffold no longer reports it. The Sonar scan of a whole check reads the same step: while it is open the scan is not run and its `sonar scan:` line names the step (`cache/sonar-scan-family.mts`), in place of the warning `CI_PARITY_SONAR_SKIPPED`. Specs: `src/service-custody.spec.mts`, `src/check-cache-sonar-scan.spec.mts`.
- Fixed: a fresh scaffold's unit specs pass: the lite passport error family names the four sign-in provider wire codes `@starci/auth` 0.6 answers (`PASSPORT_PROVIDER_STATE_INVALID`, `_REJECTED`, `_UNAVAILABLE`, `PASSPORT_PROVIDERS_INVALID`, with their kinds) and the managed sign-in service maps them to it; `readiness.service.spec.ts` expects the `HttpSecurityError` a probe throws synchronously to be the rejection itself, as the service rethrows its own family unwrapped (it asked for a `cause` the service never sets). `identity.decorators.ts` (lite) is Prettier-clean.
- Changed: `starci app add <noun>` writes every file it created or rewrote (a compose group, a controller) as the app's own prettier prints it (`formatFiles`, `sync/format.mts`; the work-tree difference of the add, `src/main.mts`), so its output passes HFS_FORMAT whatever the lengths of the names it filled in; an app with no prettier installed keeps them as written. `add cli <group>` registers the group in a cli feature root Prettier laid out one member a line. The lite migration runner names npm per platform through `dbPushCommand(platform)` (both branches covered by its spec) and its specs provide the injected values in place (BE_SPEC_FORM_ALL_ROLES). The fe starter's `AppGuideOverlay` is Prettier-clean. Specs: `src/format-files.spec.mts`, `src/queues-cli-lite.spec.mts`.
- Changed: the rest starter's OpenAPI head states that every scheme travels in `Authorization` alone and that one credential acts for one workspace.

## 6.8.0 - 2026-11-11

- Added: an api app of be may declare its contract in hfs.json, `sides.be.apps[].contract`: `graphql` (the default, also when absent; every existing app resolves and is judged exactly as before) or `rest` (`runtime/scripts/hfs/contract-kind.mts`). The declaration refuses `contract` on another side or app kind, an unknown kind, and api apps of both kinds in one back end (`HFS_DECLARATION_INVALID`).
- Added: `starci app emit` writes `contracts/<app>/openapi.json` for a rest app: the OpenAPI 3 document Nest's own `@nestjs/swagger` builds over the controllers of the app's module graph (read from source by `emit/static-graph.mts` `composeHttp`, loaded without booting the app) and the head `apps/<app>/src/openapi.ts` (`emit/rest-worker.mts`); keys are sorted so the file is a function of the code. A literal `setGlobalPrefix("<prefix>")` of `main.ts` prefixes the paths and the `controllers` of a dynamic module the source returns are read; a prefix that is not one literal and a graph importing `RouterModule` are refused (exit 1), never emitted with wrong paths. The class loader of the GraphQL worker is shared (`emit/class-loader.mts`). R23 (`HFS_CONTRACT_SNAPSHOT_DRIFT`) judges a rest app's openapi.json as it judges a schema (fresh, stale, not committed, left behind) and requires no schema.graphql of it.
- Added (R396): the committed document of a rest app holds `BE_REST_OPERATION_ID` (an explicit unique `<area>.<action>`), `BE_REST_UNTYPED` (named body and 2xx schemas), `BE_REST_ERROR_SHAPE` (one default error schema requiring code and message), `BE_REST_PAGINATION` (a list GET is a `{ items, nextCursor }` page read with `cursor` and `limit`) and `BE_REST_SECURITY` (a declared scheme; a public operation carries `x-public-reason`) (`runtime/scripts/hfs/rules/rest-contract.mts`).
- Changed (R278): a back end whose api apps declare rest refuses a GraphQL door or the GraphQL platform (`BE_REST_APP_GRAPHQL`). Changed (R177): `BE_CONTRACT_BREAKING` also judges a rest app's openapi.json against its pin `contracts/<app>/openapi.pin.json` (slot `be.contract.openapi.pin`): no removed or renamed operation, no new required parameter or body field, no removed or retyped parameter or answer field, unless info.version raises the major.
- Changed: manifest 3.33.0 (slot `be.contract.openapi.pin`; `be.app.api` allows `openapi.ts`); canon pin `@nestjs/swagger` 11.4.8 (the Nest 11 line; a devDependency here for the emit specs).
- Specs: `src/rest-contract.spec.mts` (declaration, emit and drift over a fixture app, every R396 law and R177 change on its own fixture, the R278 refusal).
- Added: a project declares its sides, hfs.json `sides` = any non-empty subset of `be`, `fe` and `packages` (`"packages": {}`, the root workspace `packages/*`); an empty set, an unknown side, a non-empty `packages` and a `reads` of an undeclared side are `HFS_DECLARATION_INVALID` (`runtime/scripts/hfs/project-sides.mts`, `declaration-shape.mts`). The app view carries `declaredSides`; an undeclared side resolves to an empty view. An app of be and fe resolves and is judged as before.
- Changed: the front end's codegen is rendered only for a project that declares fe: slot `app.scripts` names `managedWhenSides: [fe]` (a new slot key, manifest 3.33.0), so lite's managed `scripts/codegen.mjs` and the `codegen`, `codegen:graphql` and `build:fe` scripts exist exactly with fe; a full scaffold without fe starts `scripts/` with `.gitkeep`.
- Changed: `starci app scaffold` writes every side set that holds be, in both editions and both contracts (be, be+fe, be+packages, be+fe+packages); a set without be is refused `HFS_SCAFFOLD_SIDES` (lawful in hfs.json, no starter yet). Without fe the full starter's dev services and README name no fe app. Spec: `src/scaffold-side-combinations.spec.mts`.
- Fixed (R149, `BE_CLI_COMMAND_SHAPE`): nest-commander matches a sub-command by its class name, so every `starci app add cli <group>` wrote a `RunCli` and the second group's `run` ran the first one's action. The cli pattern now writes `<Group>RunCli` and the starter's migrate command is `MigrateRunCli`; `starci app check` refuses two command classes of the cli feature root that share a name (`rules/cli.mts`, both files named).
- Added: the verbs that work on one side refuse a project that does not declare it, before touching a folder (`runtime/scripts/hfs/side-gate.mts`, `SIDE_NOT_DECLARED`, exit 2): `app emit`, `app db <verb>`, `app world <action>` and `app add` of a back-end noun need be; `app add` of a front-end unit, `app add table --fe`, `app add app --kind next` and `app stack build-args` need fe; `app add module` needs packages; `app check --side <side>` needs that side. `app check` and `app sync` skip an undeclared side by the declaration. Spec: `src/side-gate.spec.mts`.
- Upgrade, per existing app: run `starci app sync --execute` once. It writes the two declarations R399 asks of an app that predates them (`sync/declared-sides.mts`; the dry run names them as `HFS_SYNC_DRIFT`): hfs.json gains `"packages": {}` under `sides` when `packages/` holds a root workspace package, and `.starcistacks/application-stacks.yaml` gains, under `schema:`, the plain-words line and `sides: {be, fe, packages}` read from hfs.json. Nothing else of either file changes.
- Added (R399, `rules/project-sides.mts`): `HFS_SIDE_FOLDER_MISSING` (a declared side tracks nothing), `HFS_SIDE_UNDECLARED` (a tracked be/, fe/ or packages/ of an undeclared side; an existing app with root packages adds `"packages": {}`), and on `.starcistacks/application-stacks.yaml` the new `sides: {be, fe, packages}` field: `STACKS_SIDES_UNDECLARED` (warning), `STACKS_SIDES_DRIFT`, `STACKS_COMPONENT_SIDE`.
- Changed: the side checks run only for a declared side: `checkRepo`, the architecture machine, required root paths (`app.sides`), slot enabling, sync's managed files, the root eslint config of root packages (no fe canon without fe), the managed scripts (`build:fe` and `codegen:graphql` only with fe), Sonar sources, the CI lint matrix and fe build steps (R301 `lint-parallel` judges the declared sides), R143 workspaces (`fe/apps/*`, `fe/packages/*` only with fe), R145 with be, BE_CLI_REQUIRED and kinds with be, R52/R59 with fe, R113 with both.
- Added: `starci app scaffold <name> --without <side[,side]>` and `--contract graphql|rest`: the scaffold writes only the declared sides, its root manifest's workspaces and dependencies for them (swagger in place of the GraphQL server for rest), a starter root package `packages/<name>-shared`, the stack declaration's `sides` field under a plain-words line (and, without fe, no web component, image, process, probe, origin, Swarm service, locked image or ingress site), a README of the declared sides, and for a rest api app `apps/<app>/src/openapi.ts` with the emitted `contracts/<app>/openapi.json` and the opt-in slot `be.contract.openapi`. Specs: `src/project-sides.spec.mts`, `src/scaffold-sides.spec.mts`.
- Added: the managed sign-in doors of a rest back end carry their OpenAPI shape (`sync/rest-doors.mts`): the auth controller of the auth-lite group renders `@ApiOperation({ operationId: "auth.<door>", security: [] })`, `x-public-reason`, named answer DTOs and one default `ErrorResponse` when the api apps declare rest; a graphql back end renders it byte for byte as before. A rest scaffold overlays `templates/be/skeleton-rest` (swagger-described health doors, `apps/<app>/src/openapi.ts`, the emitted `contracts/<app>/openapi.json`), so R396 holds on a fresh rest app. The rest overlay also holds the HTTP-only `platform/http-security/execution-request.mapper.ts` with its spec (a rest app installs no `@nestjs/graphql`) and a `main.ts` that serves the document over the same head (`SwaggerModule.setup("docs", ...)`); the doors' response DTOs carry member JSDoc and no literal response copy. The starter root package is CommonJS (`@starci/tsconfig/package-cjs.json`, `tsconfig.build.json` for its build) with its constant in `src/project-name.ts`, a named re-export in `src/index.ts` and a spec beside it. `starci app check --gate lint` lints only the declared app sides.
- Added (R400, `runtime/scripts/hfs/rules/data-repository.mts`): a project's business data lives in git only in its private data repository `<project>-data`. The hfs check of an app refuses a tracked document of the data format, a data manifest (`format: "<name>-data@<major>"` with `files`) or a record file (`schema: "<name>-data/<entity>@<major>"` with `items`, plaintext or sealed) (`HFS_DATA_IN_CODE`), a package.json dependency or workspace, a submodule or a tsconfig/jsconfig path naming `<project>-data` (`HFS_DATA_DEPENDENCY`), and an `application-stacks.yaml` `data` block (new optional field `data: {repository, private}`) that names another repository or is not private (`STACKS_DATA_REPOSITORY`). The commit hook's guard (`starci app check --staged`, `sync/hygiene.mts`) gains the data guard: a staged document of the format is refused in any repository but a data repository (a root `manifest.json` naming the format and no hfs.json), and there every file of the indexed `data/` must be a sops envelope (`HFS_DATA_UNSEALED`) listed by the manifest with its item count, a hex sha256 and a schema id of the format (`HFS_DATA_MANIFEST_DRIFT`). Spec: `src/data-repository.spec.mts`.
- Fixed: the sops envelope test (`runtime/scripts/lib/sops-envelope.mts`) accepts an empty-string leaf, which sops leaves as it is; a sealed file holding one was refused as no envelope.

## 6.7.1 - 2026-11-10

- Added: a product's concepts are Work of both editions. Slot `app.starciwork` admits `concepts/index.yaml` (the catalog, `work/concept-catalog@1`) and `concepts/<name>/index.yaml` (one concept, `work/concept@1`), under lite as well (manifest 3.29.0); `contexts/project/work.md` PROJECT-WORK-7 says what a concept holds, that it is read before designing, written when the owner rules, and that a contradiction is raised. `isProductPath` and `isLiteProductPath` (`runtime/scripts/lib/starciwork-boundary.mts`) admit the two paths and nothing else below `concepts/`, so a lite Work reader opens them; the lite scan of local Work admits a directory whose own `index.yaml` the slot admits (`liteStarciworkFinding`, `runtime/scripts/hfs/path-findings.mts`). The `.starciwork/.gitignore` templates of both editions re-admit `/concepts/`. Spec: `src/hfs-starciwork-lite-forbidden.spec.mts` (the concept records are legal tracked and untracked, any other file of a lite concept folder is `HFS_STARCIWORK_LITE_FORBIDDEN`).
- Changed: the words of `HFS_STARCIWORK_LITE_FORBIDDEN` name the concept records beside `workspace.yaml` and `brand/`.
- Upgrade, per existing app that writes concepts: `starci app sync --execute` renders the `.starciwork/.gitignore` that re-admits `concepts/`; until then git ignores the folder.

## 6.7.0 - 2026-11-09

- Changed (R395, manifest 3.28.0): the gallery is organised per screen. `gallery/pages/<area>/<screen>/` (one agent, one state) holds exactly `1440.png`, `1920.png`, `390.png`, `explain.md` and an optional `links.json`, and `gallery/flows.json` names flows; any other file or folder (`1440-open.png`, `sales-1440.png`, `before/`) is `HFS_APP_GALLERY_SCREEN_FILE`; the explanation is the `explain.md` (`contexts/project/gallery.md` PROJECT-GALLERY-2): front matter `title`, `route`, `area`, and one `## <region name>` per visible region with the lines `Vì sao:`, `Luật:` and `Grammar:`. `ruleParams.gallery.shots` is now `pages` (`gallery/pages`) and `ignoredShots` is `pages/**/*.png`; the managed root `.gitignore` block ignores `gallery/pages/**/*.png` (the `explain.md` files are source). The slot `app.gallery` also lists `vite.config.js` and `src/explain.js`; the new slots `app.gallery-explain` (the tracked `gallery/pages/**/{explain.md,links.json}`) and `app.gallery-flows` (`gallery/flows.json`) place the source beside the ignored shots. The earlier `gallery/shots/` layout is gone. The manifest stays on the 3.x line (3.28.0) although this changes the paths of slots 3.27.0 introduced: the gallery shipped hours earlier in 6.6.0 and no app consumes it yet.
- Added: the check codes `HFS_APP_GALLERY_EXPLAIN_MISSING` (a page folder has shots and no `explain.md`; the shots are read from the work tree), `HFS_APP_GALLERY_REGION_INCOMPLETE` (no region, or a region without a `Vì sao` or `Grammar` value), `HFS_APP_GALLERY_REGION_UNCLEAR` (a value starting with `CHƯA RÕ` or `SAI`; `localOnly`, so `--env local` reports it and `--env prod` blocks), `HFS_APP_GALLERY_FRONT_MATTER` (a warning), `HFS_APP_GALLERY_SCREEN_FILE` (a file or folder a screen folder does not allow) and `HFS_APP_GALLERY_LINKS` (a `links.json` or `flows.json` that is malformed or names a missing screen). The reader is `scripts/hfs/gallery-explain.mts`.
- Changed: the managed gallery app shows each page's shots beside its explanation, flags the pages and regions the gate flags, and speaks the app's default locale (new labels in `GALLERY_LABELS`). The viewer shows only the screens that have an `explain.md`, draws `links.json` hotspots over the shot and has a flow map view ("Bản đồ luồng"). New managed files `src/explain.js` (the same reader in plain JavaScript; `src/app-gallery.spec.mts` runs both over the same texts) and `vite.config.js` (the dev server does not watch the PNGs).
- Fixed: the front-end world analysis no longer throws `No type checker holds <file>` when a component calls a variable a workspace package aliases to another binding (`export const useThing = implementation`, reached through a tsconfig path alias): `symbolIsWorld` and `namespaceIsWorld` treat a file outside the checked graph as outside the app's world, as `functionUsesWorld` already did. Spec: `src/fe-world-workspace-source.spec.mts`. An app can drop any local patch of `frontend-world.mjs`.
- Fixed (cli): `starci deps publish-local` no longer refuses `DEPS_INSTALL_LINKED` in a worktree whose `node_modules` links an install that already satisfies its lockfile.
- Upgrade, per existing app: 1. `starci app sync --execute` rewrites `gallery/` and the `.gitignore` block; 2. move each `gallery/shots/<area>/<page>__<width>__after.png` to `gallery/pages/<area>/<page>/<width>.png` (the `__before` shots and widths other than 1440, 1920 and 390 are dropped); 3. write each page's `explain.md`; 4. commit `gallery/` with the `explain.md` files, never a PNG.

## 6.6.2 - 2026-11-09

- Changed: the canon pins name the Grammar family 0.37.2.

## 6.6.1 - 2026-11-09

- Changed: the canon pins name the Grammar family 0.37.0.

## 6.6.0 - 2026-11-09

- Added (R395): every app source repository carries a screenshot gallery at `gallery/` (`contexts/project/gallery.md` PROJECT-GALLERY-1). It is a standalone Vite app that shows `gallery/shots/<area>/<page>__<width>__<before|after>.png` (1440 and 390 per page, before and after each UI fix round), served on `local.basePort` + 50 with a pinned Vite. Its files are managed by `starci app sync` (slot `app.gallery`, manifest 3.27.0, optional so a pinned repository stays green; `ruleParams.gallery` holds the folder, the `dev` script, the offset 50, the Vite pin and the ignored shots pattern), so a scaffold writes it, a sync adds it to an existing app and a hand edit is `HFS_MANAGED_FILE_DRIFT`; the managed root `.gitignore` block ignores `gallery/shots/**/*.png`. The full skeleton declares `local.basePort: 3000`, the base its ports already follow.
- Added: the check codes `HFS_APP_GALLERY_MISSING` (no `gallery/`, or a managed file of it absent), `HFS_APP_GALLERY_SCRIPT` (no `dev` script `vite --port <base + 50> --strictPort`, or no `local.basePort` to derive it from), `HFS_APP_GALLERY_VITE_UNPINNED` and `HFS_APP_GALLERY_SHOTS_TRACKED` (the shot PNGs are not git-ignored, or some are tracked); `HFS_SERVICE_PLACEMENT` (R163) leaves the gallery's `package.json` alone.
- Added: `starci app stack up|down|status|logs|verify --env <env>` run the gallery as a native process on base + 50 next to web and api, with a one-time `npm install` in `gallery/`, a readiness row and an origin; a process an environment declares itself under the name `gallery` is kept (`withGallery`, `stack/local-config.mts`).
- Added: the managed gallery speaks the app's default locale. Its words (search box, `All`, the empty state, the before and after labels, the document title and language, the README) come from the `DEFAULT_LOCALE` of the app's i18n routing source (`fe/packages/<project>-i18n/src/routing.ts`, or `fe/apps/<app>/src/modules/i18n/routing.ts` of a lite app): `vi` renders Vietnamese, `en` English, and any other locale or none declared falls back to `en` (`GALLERY_LABELS`, `galleryLocale`, `declaredDefaultLocale` in `scripts/hfs/gallery.mts`). The files stay managed per locale, so an app whose default locale is `vi` is never rewritten to English by `starci app sync`. Specs: `src/app-gallery.spec.mts` (a `vi` app renders Vietnamese labels, an `en` app English ones, `fr` falls back to `en`, a changed default locale is drift that sync settles).
- Upgrade, per existing app (R395 is reported by `starci app check` as `HFS_APP_GALLERY_MISSING` until the gallery exists): 1. `starci app sync --execute` lays down `gallery/` and the `gallery/shots/**/*.png` line of the managed `.gitignore` block (an app with no `local.basePort` declares one first, R389); 2. `npm install` in `gallery/` (or `starci app stack up`) writes `gallery/package-lock.json`; 3. commit `gallery/` with its lockfile, never `gallery/shots/`.

An app that needs glyphs adds its own `Icon` leaf (FE-ICON).

## 6.5.5 - 2026-11-09

- Changed: the canon pins name `@starci/grammar` 0.32.1.

- Fixed: the front-end world analysis (`FE_WORLD_OWNER_RENDER_BOUNDARY`, `FE_COMPONENT_WORLD_OWNERSHIP`, `FE_BLOCK_PRODUCT_HOOK_DEFINITION`, and through them the canon-fe rules built on it) no longer throws `No type checker holds <file>` when a component calls a function of a workspace package's source reached through a tsconfig path alias (nivo: `@nivo-lite/blocks-core` -> `packages/blocks-core/src/index.ts`). The type context keeps only files under the fe root, so no checker holds that file. `functionUsesWorld` now treats a function declared outside the checked graph as outside the app's world and does not descend into it: the package is its own unit, judged by its own lint, and the walk neither crashes nor reports it. Nothing is loosened: every function of the fe tree is judged as before.
- Specs: `src/fe-world-workspace-source.spec.mts` (lite and full: a component calling a package source through a path alias is analysed without error and reports nothing).
